decision = {
	id = 2300
	major = no
	persistent = yes

	name = "Move Capital Back"
	desc = "Ever since being evicted from our ancient capital, we have endevoured to return, and to return back home we shall."

	potential = {
		OR = {
			tag = ABB
			tag = IRA
			tag = CAL
			tag = MES
		}
		OR = {
			owned = { province = 489 data = -1 }
			NOT = {
				owned = { province = 489 data = -1 }
			}
		}
	}

	trigger = {
			owned = { province = 489 data = -1 }
			control = { province = 489 data = -1 }
			NOT = {
				capital = 489
		}
	}

	ai_trigger = {}

	action = {
		command = { type = capital which = 489 }
		command = { type = treasury value = -20 }
	}
}
